gml_Script_anon@72@gml_Object_sys_imgui_Create_0

gml_Script_anon@43@gml_Object_sys_imgui_Step_0
__shd_outline
#define LOWPREC lowp\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main() {\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4(in_Position, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n	\n    v_vColour	= in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n\n\n#define MAX_THICKNESS 8.0\n\nvarying vec2	v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4	v_vColour;\n\nuniform vec4	u_line_color;\nuniform vec2	u_pixel_size;\nuniform vec3	u_thk_rn_tol;	// Thickness, roundness, tolerance\nuniform vec4	u_uv;\n\n\nfloat point_in_elipse(vec2 center, vec2 point, vec2 axis) {\n	// Turn on again if scales are glitchy, but it mostly unlikely\n	//vec2 semi_axis = vec2(max(axis.x, axis.y), min(axis.x, axis.y));\n	\n    float dist = (pow((point.x - center.x), 2.0) / pow(axis.x, 2.0))\n			   + (pow((point.y - center.y), 2.0) / pow(axis.y, 2.0));\n    return dist;\n}\n\n\nvoid main() {\n	\n	vec4 frag_col		= texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n	float thickness		= min(abs(u_thk_rn_tol.x), MAX_THICKNESS);\n	float tolerance		= clamp(u_thk_rn_tol.z, 0.0, 0.999999);\n	float outline		= 0.0;\n	bool in_line		= u_thk_rn_tol.x < 0.0;\n	bool stop			= false;\n	\n	// Invert the condition for inline cases\n	if (frag_col.a <= tolerance ^^ in_line) {\n		for (float xx = -MAX_THICKNESS; xx <= MAX_THICKNESS; xx++) {\n			if (xx < -thickness) {continue;}\n			if (xx > thickness) {break;}\n			\n			for (float yy = -MAX_THICKNESS; yy <= MAX_THICKNESS; yy++) {					\n				if (yy < -thickness) {continue;}\n				if (yy > thickness) {break;}\n				\n				vec2 loop_pos = vec2(xx, yy) * u_pixel_size;\n				vec2 chk_pos = v_vTexcoord + loop_pos;\n				\n				// Sample all surrouding pixels - a two pass system would be better for thicker outlines\n				vec4 chk_col = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, chk_pos);	\n				\n				// Same inverted condition stuff\n				if (chk_col.a > tolerance ^^ in_line) {\n					\n					// Check if the UV still on the sprite area\n					bool out_bound = (chk_pos.x < u_uv.r || chk_pos.y < u_uv.g || chk_pos.x > u_uv.b || chk_pos.y > u_uv.a);\n					vec2 axis = u_pixel_size.xy * thickness;\n					\n					// Elipse because texture page can be rectangular - maybe there is a better way but this does the job\n					float dist = point_in_elipse(v_vTexcoord, chk_pos, axis);\n					if (dist <= 2.0-u_thk_rn_tol.y && !out_bound) {\n						outline = 1.0;\n						stop = true;\n						break;\n					}\n				}\n			}\n			if (stop) {\n				break;\n			}\n		}\n	}\n	\n	// Defines the final fragment color as the sampled or as the outline\n	gl_FragColor = mix(v_vColour * frag_col, u_line_color, outline);\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main() {\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4(in_Position, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n	\n    v_vColour	= in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n\n\n#define MAX_THICKNESS 8.0\n\nvarying vec2	v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4	v_vColour;\n\nuniform vec4	u_line_color;\nuniform vec2	u_pixel_size;\nuniform vec3	u_thk_rn_tol;	// Thickness, roundness, tolerance\nuniform vec4	u_uv;\n\n\nfloat point_in_elipse(vec2 center, vec2 point, vec2 axis) {\n	// Turn on again if scales are glitchy, but it mostly unlikely\n	//vec2 semi_axis = vec2(max(axis.x, axis.y), min(axis.x, axis.y));\n	\n    float dist = (pow((point.x - center.x), 2.0) / pow(axis.x, 2.0))\n			   + (pow((point.y - center.y), 2.0) / pow(axis.y, 2.0));\n    return dist;\n}\n\n\nvoid main() {\n	\n	vec4 frag_col		= texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n	float thickness		= min(abs(u_thk_rn_tol.x), MAX_THICKNESS);\n	float tolerance		= clamp(u_thk_rn_tol.z, 0.0, 0.999999);\n	float outline		= 0.0;\n	bool in_line		= u_thk_rn_tol.x < 0.0;\n	bool stop			= false;\n	\n	// Invert the condition for inline cases\n	if (frag_col.a <= tolerance ^^ in_line) {\n		for (float xx = -MAX_THICKNESS; xx <= MAX_THICKNESS; xx++) {\n			if (xx < -thickness) {continue;}\n			if (xx > thickness) {break;}\n			\n			for (float yy = -MAX_THICKNESS; yy <= MAX_THICKNESS; yy++) {					\n				if (yy < -thickness) {continue;}\n				if (yy > thickness) {break;}\n				\n				vec2 loop_pos = vec2(xx, yy) * u_pixel_size;\n				vec2 chk_pos = v_vTexcoord + loop_pos;\n				\n				// Sample all surrouding pixels - a two pass system would be better for thicker outlines\n				vec4 chk_col = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, chk_pos);	\n				\n				// Same inverted condition stuff\n				if (chk_col.a > tolerance ^^ in_line) {\n					\n					// Check if the UV still on the sprite area\n					bool out_bound = (chk_pos.x < u_uv.r || chk_pos.y < u_uv.g || chk_pos.x > u_uv.b || chk_pos.y > u_uv.a);\n					vec2 axis = u_pixel_size.xy * thickness;\n					\n					// Elipse because texture page can be rectangular - maybe there is a better way but this does the job\n					float dist = point_in_elipse(v_vTexcoord, chk_pos, axis);\n					if (dist <= 2.0-u_thk_rn_tol.y && !out_bound) {\n						outline = 1.0;\n						stop = true;\n						break;\n					}\n				}\n			}\n			if (stop) {\n				break;\n			}\n		}\n	}\n	\n	// Defines the final fragment color as the sampled or as the outline\n	gl_FragColor = mix(v_vColour * frag_col, u_line_color, outline);\n}\n
#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nfloat4x4 	gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX] : register(c0);\n\nbool 	gm_LightingEnabled;\nbool 	gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nfloat 	gm_FogStart;\nfloat 	gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nfloat4 gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nfloat4 gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// normalised direction, W is 1 if directional light is active or 0 otherwise\nfloat4 gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nfloat4 gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];				// rgb=colour, a=1\n\n
// GameMaker reserved and common types/inputs\n\nsampler2D gm_BaseTexture : register(S0);\n\nbool 	gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nfloat4 	gm_FogColour;\nbool 	gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nfloat4	gm_AlphaRefValue;\n

#define LOWPREC lowp\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColor;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColor = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\nconst float PI = 3.14159265359;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColor;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vTexel;\nuniform vec2 u_vShadowDelta;\n\nconst int u_iOutlineSamples = 4;\nconst int u_iOutlineSize    = 1;\n\nfloat InsideTexture(vec2 point)\n{\n    vec2 result = step(0.0, point) - step(1.0, point);\n    return result.x*result.y;   \n}\n\nfloat SampleAlpha(vec2 inTexcoord)\n{\n    vec2  texcoord;\n    float alpha = 0.0;\n    \n    for(int iAngle = 0; iAngle < u_iOutlineSamples; iAngle++)\n    {\n        float angle = 2.0*PI*float(iAngle) / float(u_iOutlineSamples);\n        for(int radius = 1; radius <= u_iOutlineSize; radius++)\n        {\n            texcoord = inTexcoord + u_vTexel*(float(radius)*vec2(cos(angle), sin(angle)));\n            alpha = mix(alpha, 1.0, texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, texcoord).a*InsideTexture(texcoord));\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return alpha;\n}\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    if (all(equal(u_vShadowDelta, vec2(0.0))))\n    {\n        gl_FragColor = vec4(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord).a,\n                            SampleAlpha(v_vTexcoord),\n                            0.0,\n                            1.0);\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        gl_FragColor = vec4(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord).a,\n                            SampleAlpha(v_vTexcoord),\n                            SampleAlpha(v_vTexcoord - u_vTexel*u_vShadowDelta),\n                            1.0);\n    }\n}\n

gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColor;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColor = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\nconst float PI = 3.14159265359;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColor;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vTexel;\nuniform vec2 u_vShadowDelta;\n\nconst int u_iOutlineSamples = 4;\nconst int u_iOutlineSize    = 1;\n\nfloat InsideTexture(vec2 point)\n{\n    vec2 result = step(0.0, point) - step(1.0, point);\n    return result.x*result.y;   \n}\n\nfloat SampleAlpha(vec2 inTexcoord)\n{\n    vec2  texcoord;\n    float alpha = 0.0;\n    \n    for(int iAngle = 0; iAngle < u_iOutlineSamples; iAngle++)\n    {\n        float angle = 2.0*PI*float(iAngle) / float(u_iOutlineSamples);\n        for(int radius = 1; radius <= u_iOutlineSize; radius++)\n        {\n            texcoord = inTexcoord + u_vTexel*(float(radius)*vec2(cos(angle), sin(angle)));\n            alpha = mix(alpha, 1.0, texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, texcoord).a*InsideTexture(texcoord));\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return alpha;\n}\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    if (all(equal(u_vShadowDelta, vec2(0.0))))\n    {\n        gl_FragColor = vec4(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord).a,\n                            SampleAlpha(v_vTexcoord),\n                            0.0,\n                            1.0);\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        gl_FragColor = vec4(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord).a,\n                            SampleAlpha(v_vTexcoord),\n                            SampleAlpha(v_vTexcoord - u_vTexel*u_vShadowDelta),\n                            1.0);\n    }\n}\n

__shd_scribble_bake_outline_4dir
#define LOWPREC lowp\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n//   @jujuadams   v8.0.0   2021-12-15\nprecision highp float;\n\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColor;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColor = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n//   @jujuadams   v8.0.0   2021-12-15\nprecision highp float;\n\nconst float PI = 3.14159265359;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColor;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vTexel;\nuniform vec3 u_vOutlineColor;\n\nconst int u_iOutlineSamples = 4;\nconst int u_iOutlineSize    = 1;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 outlineColor = vec4(u_vOutlineColor, 1.0);\n    vec4 newColor = vec4(u_vOutlineColor, 0.0);\n    \n    for(int iAngle = 0; iAngle < u_iOutlineSamples; iAngle++)\n    {\n        float fAngle = 2.0*PI*float(iAngle) / float(u_iOutlineSamples);\n        for(int radius = 1; radius <= u_iOutlineSize; radius++)\n        {\n            newColor = mix(newColor, outlineColor, texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord + u_vTexel*(float(radius)*vec2(cos(fAngle), sin(fAngle)))).a);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    vec4 sample = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n    gl_FragColor = v_vColor*mix(newColor, sample, sample.a);\n    gl_FragColor.a = max(newColor.a,sample.a);\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n//   @jujuadams   v8.0.0   2021-12-15\nprecision highp float;\n\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColor;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColor = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
