ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough vertex shader\n//\nattribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)\n//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)\n\nvarying vec2 v_vPos;\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n    v_vPos = in_Position.xy;\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n#define Transparent vec4(.0,.0,.0,.0)\n#define Tolerance 0.004 \n\nuniform vec2 u_pixelSize;\nuniform vec4 u_Uvs;\nuniform sampler2D u_palTexture;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\nvarying vec2 v_vPos;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vLightPos;\nuniform vec2 u_vOffset;\nuniform float u_fLightRadius;\n\nuniform sampler2D u_sGame;\n\nvec4 findAltColor(vec4 inCol, vec2 corner, float u_paletteId)\n{\n    if(inCol.a == 0.) return Transparent;\n    \n    float dist;\n    vec2 testPos;\n    vec4 leftCol;\n    for(float i = corner.y; i < u_Uvs.w; i+=u_pixelSize.y )\n    {\n			testPos = vec2(corner.x,i);\n      leftCol = texture2D( u_palTexture, testPos);\n        \n			dist = distance(leftCol,inCol);\n\n			if(dist < Tolerance)\n      {\n				testPos = vec2(corner.x + u_pixelSize.x * floor(u_paletteId + 1.0), i);\n        return mix(texture2D(u_palTexture, vec2(testPos.x - u_pixelSize.x, testPos.y)), texture2D(u_palTexture, testPos), fract(u_paletteId));\n      }\n    }\n    return inCol;\n}\n\nfloat getDitherValue(vec2 pixelPos) {\n    vec2 bayerCoord = floor(mod(pixelPos, 4.0));\n    int x = int(bayerCoord.x);\n    int y = int(bayerCoord.y);\n\n    //  4x4 Bayer Matrix row definitions\n    vec4 row0 = vec4( 0.0, 12.0,  3.0, 15.0);\n    vec4 row1 = vec4( 8.0,  4.0, 11.0,  7.0);\n    vec4 row2 = vec4( 2.0, 14.0,  1.0, 13.0);\n    vec4 row3 = vec4(10.0,  6.0,  9.0,  5.0);\n\n    vec4 activeRow = vec4(0.0);\n    if (y == 0) activeRow = row0;\n    else if (y == 1) activeRow = row1;\n    else if (y == 2) activeRow = row2;\n    else if (y == 3) activeRow = row3;\n\n    float value = 0.0;\n    if (x == 0) value = activeRow.x;\n    else if (x == 1) value = activeRow.y;\n    else if (x == 2) value = activeRow.z;\n    else if (x == 3) value = activeRow.w;\n\n    return value / 16.0;\n}\n\nconst bool USE_DITHER = true;\n\nfloat get_alpha(vec4 col){\n	return float(col.b > 0.01);\n}\n\nuniform vec2 u_vScreenSize;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	vec2 sample = v_vPos / u_vScreenSize;\n	\n    gl_FragColor = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, sample );\n	\n	if(gl_FragColor.a == 0.)\n		discard;\n		\n	vec2 texelX = vec2(1. / u_vScreenSize.x, 0.);\n	vec2 texelY = vec2(0., 1. / u_vScreenSize.y);\n\n    float alphaLeft  = get_alpha(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, sample - texelX));\n    float alphaRight = get_alpha(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, sample + texelX));\n    float alphaUp    = get_alpha(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, sample - texelY));\n    float alphaDown  = get_alpha(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, sample + texelY));\n	float outline = min(\n		min(alphaLeft, alphaUp),\n		min(alphaRight,alphaDown)\n	);\n	\n	float light_size = u_fLightRadius;\n	vec2 light_pos = u_vLightPos;\n	\n	vec2 centered = v_vPos - light_pos;\n	\n	float dist = length(centered);\n	vec2 normal = normalize(centered);\n	\n	float face = dot(normal, gl_FragColor.rg*2. - 1.);\n	float light = max(0., 1. - dist/(light_size));\n	float light1 = max(0., 1. - dist/(light_size*.4));\n	\n	float threshold = getDitherValue(v_vPos + u_vOffset);\n	\n	//float light_2 = (1. - dot(normal, vec2(0.))) * max(0., light*3. - 2.) * .5;\n	\n	//float final_light = max(\n	//	light_2 > threshold ? 1. : 0.,\n	//	face * (light > threshold ? 1. : 0.)\n	//);\n	\n	float half_a = outline > .5 ? .5 : 1.;\n	\n	float final_light = face * (USE_DITHER ? (light * 2. > threshold ? 1. : 0.) : max(0., light*2. - 1.));\n	float highlight = (light1 > threshold ? 1. : 0.);\n	\n	if(highlight == 0. && final_light < .1)\n		discard;\n	\n    gl_FragColor = texture2D( u_sGame, sample);\n	vec4 new_col = findAltColor(gl_FragColor, u_Uvs.xy, 1. + (final_light < .1 ? 0. : 1. - outline));\n	if(distance(gl_FragColor.rgb,new_col.rgb) < .001)\n		discard;\n	\n	gl_FragColor = new_col;\n	//if(final_light < .1)\n	//	gl_FragColor = vec4(0.,1.,1.,1.);\n	\n	//gl_FragColor.rgb = u_vLightColor/256. * half_a;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough vertex shader\n//\nattribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)\n//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)\n\nvarying vec2 v_vPos;\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n    v_vPos = in_Position.xy;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n#define Transparent vec4(.0,.0,.0,.0)\n#define Tolerance 0.004 \n\nuniform vec2 u_pixelSize;\nuniform vec4 u_Uvs;\nuniform sampler2D u_palTexture;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\nvarying vec2 v_vPos;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vLightPos;\nuniform vec2 u_vOffset;\nuniform float u_fLightRadius;\n\nuniform sampler2D u_sGame;\n\nvec4 findAltColor(vec4 inCol, vec2 corner, float u_paletteId)\n{\n    if(inCol.a == 0.) return Transparent;\n    \n    float dist;\n    vec2 testPos;\n    vec4 leftCol;\n    for(float i = corner.y; i < u_Uvs.w; i+=u_pixelSize.y )\n    {\n			testPos = vec2(corner.x,i);\n      leftCol = texture2D( u_palTexture, testPos);\n        \n			dist = distance(leftCol,inCol);\n\n			if(dist < Tolerance)\n      {\n				testPos = vec2(corner.x + u_pixelSize.x * floor(u_paletteId + 1.0), i);\n        return mix(texture2D(u_palTexture, vec2(testPos.x - u_pixelSize.x, testPos.y)), texture2D(u_palTexture, testPos), fract(u_paletteId));\n      }\n    }\n    return inCol;\n}\n\nfloat getDitherValue(vec2 pixelPos) {\n    vec2 bayerCoord = floor(mod(pixelPos, 4.0));\n    int x = int(bayerCoord.x);\n    int y = int(bayerCoord.y);\n\n    //  4x4 Bayer Matrix row definitions\n    vec4 row0 = vec4( 0.0, 12.0,  3.0, 15.0);\n    vec4 row1 = vec4( 8.0,  4.0, 11.0,  7.0);\n    vec4 row2 = vec4( 2.0, 14.0,  1.0, 13.0);\n    vec4 row3 = vec4(10.0,  6.0,  9.0,  5.0);\n\n    vec4 activeRow = vec4(0.0);\n    if (y == 0) activeRow = row0;\n    else if (y == 1) activeRow = row1;\n    else if (y == 2) activeRow = row2;\n    else if (y == 3) activeRow = row3;\n\n    float value = 0.0;\n    if (x == 0) value = activeRow.x;\n    else if (x == 1) value = activeRow.y;\n    else if (x == 2) value = activeRow.z;\n    else if (x == 3) value = activeRow.w;\n\n    return value / 16.0;\n}\n\nconst bool USE_DITHER = true;\n\nfloat get_alpha(vec4 col){\n	return float(col.b > 0.01);\n}\n\nuniform vec2 u_vScreenSize;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	vec2 sample = v_vPos / u_vScreenSize;\n	\n    gl_FragColor = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, sample );\n	\n	if(gl_FragColor.a == 0.)\n		discard;\n		\n	vec2 texelX = vec2(1. / u_vScreenSize.x, 0.);\n	vec2 texelY = vec2(0., 1. / u_vScreenSize.y);\n\n    float alphaLeft  = get_alpha(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, sample - texelX));\n    float alphaRight = get_alpha(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, sample + texelX));\n    float alphaUp    = get_alpha(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, sample - texelY));\n    float alphaDown  = get_alpha(texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, sample + texelY));\n	float outline = min(\n		min(alphaLeft, alphaUp),\n		min(alphaRight,alphaDown)\n	);\n	\n	float light_size = u_fLightRadius;\n	vec2 light_pos = u_vLightPos;\n	\n	vec2 centered = v_vPos - light_pos;\n	\n	float dist = length(centered);\n	vec2 normal = normalize(centered);\n	\n	float face = dot(normal, gl_FragColor.rg*2. - 1.);\n	float light = max(0., 1. - dist/(light_size));\n	float light1 = max(0., 1. - dist/(light_size*.4));\n	\n	float threshold = getDitherValue(v_vPos + u_vOffset);\n	\n	//float light_2 = (1. - dot(normal, vec2(0.))) * max(0., light*3. - 2.) * .5;\n	\n	//float final_light = max(\n	//	light_2 > threshold ? 1. : 0.,\n	//	face * (light > threshold ? 1. : 0.)\n	//);\n	\n	float half_a = outline > .5 ? .5 : 1.;\n	\n	float final_light = face * (USE_DITHER ? (light * 2. > threshold ? 1. : 0.) : max(0., light*2. - 1.));\n	float highlight = (light1 > threshold ? 1. : 0.);\n	\n	if(highlight == 0. && final_light < .1)\n		discard;\n	\n    gl_FragColor = texture2D( u_sGame, sample);\n	vec4 new_col = findAltColor(gl_FragColor, u_Uvs.xy, 1. + (final_light < .1 ? 0. : 1. - outline));\n	if(distance(gl_FragColor.rgb,new_col.rgb) < .001)\n		discard;\n	\n	gl_FragColor = new_col;\n	//if(final_light < .1)\n	//	gl_FragColor = vec4(0.,1.,1.,1.);\n	\n	//gl_FragColor.rgb = u_vLightColor/256. * half_a;\n}\n
shd_lighting_process_levels
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough fragment shader\n//\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec2 v_vPos;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vLightPos;\nuniform vec2 u_vLightRadius;\nuniform float u_fLightHighlightOnly;\nuniform float u_fGlobalLightLevel;\n\n//uniform vec2 u_vScreenSize;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	//vec2 sample = v_vPos / u_vScreenSize;\n	\n	float light_size = u_vLightRadius.x;\n	float light_fade_size = u_vLightRadius.y*2.;\n	vec2 light_pos = u_vLightPos;\n	\n	vec2 centered = v_vPos - light_pos;\n	\n	float dist = max(0., length(centered) - light_size);\n	//vec2 light_normal = normalize(centered);\n	//\n    //vec2 screen_normal = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, sample );\n	//float face = gl_FragColor.b < 0.01 ? 0. : dot(light_normal, screen_normal.xy*2. - 1.);\n	float light = max(0., 1. - dist/light_fade_size) * u_fGlobalLightLevel;// + face*.5;\n	\n	float light_spaced = light*5.;\n	float light_stepped = floor(light_spaced*3.);\n	\n	//float light_gap = abs(light_stepped - light_spaced));// light_stepped*4.+1. - light_spaced);\n	\n	//light = (light_stepped + light_gap)/4.;\n	\n	float light_edge = float(mod(floor(light_spaced*3. + 1.), 3.) == 1.) * (1. - fract(light_spaced*3.));\n	\n	light = (floor(light_spaced) - light_edge);\n	\n	light = u_fLightHighlightOnly > .5 ? min(1., light) : light;\n	\n	light = light/4.;\n	\n	gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(light),1.);\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough fragment shader\n//\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec2 v_vPos;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vLightPos;\nuniform vec2 u_vLightRadius;\nuniform float u_fLightHighlightOnly;\nuniform float u_fGlobalLightLevel;\n\n//uniform vec2 u_vScreenSize;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	//vec2 sample = v_vPos / u_vScreenSize;\n	\n	float light_size = u_vLightRadius.x;\n	float light_fade_size = u_vLightRadius.y*2.;\n	vec2 light_pos = u_vLightPos;\n	\n	vec2 centered = v_vPos - light_pos;\n	\n	float dist = max(0., length(centered) - light_size);\n	//vec2 light_normal = normalize(centered);\n	//\n    //vec2 screen_normal = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, sample );\n	//float face = gl_FragColor.b < 0.01 ? 0. : dot(light_normal, screen_normal.xy*2. - 1.);\n	float light = max(0., 1. - dist/light_fade_size) * u_fGlobalLightLevel;// + face*.5;\n	\n	float light_spaced = light*5.;\n	float light_stepped = floor(light_spaced*3.);\n	\n	//float light_gap = abs(light_stepped - light_spaced));// light_stepped*4.+1. - light_spaced);\n	\n	//light = (light_stepped + light_gap)/4.;\n	\n	float light_edge = float(mod(floor(light_spaced*3. + 1.), 3.) == 1.) * (1. - fract(light_spaced*3.));\n	\n	light = (floor(light_spaced) - light_edge);\n	\n	light = u_fLightHighlightOnly > .5 ? min(1., light) : light;\n	\n	light = light/4.;\n	\n	gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(light),1.);\n}\n
shd_lighting_sprites_process_outlines
#define LOWPREC lowp\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough vertex shader\n//\nattribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)\n//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n#extension GL_OES_standard_derivatives : enable\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vTexel; \n\nuniform bool u_bIgnoreBlack;\n\nfloat get_alpha(vec4 col){\n	return float(col.a != 0.);//(u_bIgnoreBlack || length(col.rgb) > 0.01) && \n}\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	    // 1. Calculate how the texture coordinates change across screen X and Y\n    vec2 dTexX = vec2(dFdx(v_vTexcoord.x), dFdx(v_vTexcoord.y));\n    vec2 dTexY = vec2(dFdy(v_vTexcoord.x), dFdy(v_vTexcoord.y));\n\n    // 2. Normalize these to create a rotation matrix matching the screen orientation\n    //vec2 screenRight = normalize(dTexX);\n    //vec2 screenDown  = normalize(dTexY);\n\n    // 3. Transform the base texel dimensions into dynamically rotated screen offsets\n    // This scales the directional vectors based on the sprite's texture dimensions\n    vec2 rotTexelX = dTexX*3.;\n    vec2 rotTexelY = dTexY*3.;\n\n	vec4 colCenter = v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n    float alphaCenter = get_alpha(colCenter);\n	\n	if(alphaCenter == 0.)\n		discard;\n    \n    float alphaLeft  = get_alpha(v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord - rotTexelX));\n    float alphaRight = get_alpha(v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord + rotTexelX));\n    float alphaUp    = get_alpha(v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord - rotTexelY));\n    float alphaDown  = get_alpha(v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord + rotTexelY));\n	float outline = min(\n		min(alphaLeft, alphaUp),\n		min(alphaRight,alphaDown)\n	);\n	\n    float diffLeft  = (alphaCenter - alphaLeft);\n    float diffRight = (alphaCenter - alphaRight);\n    float diffUp    = (alphaCenter - alphaUp);\n    float diffDown  = (alphaCenter - alphaDown);\n	\n    float horizontalTotal = (alphaLeft == 0. && alphaLeft == alphaRight) ? diffLeft : diffLeft - diffRight;\n    float verticalTotal   = (alphaUp == 0. && alphaUp == alphaDown) ? diffUp : diffUp - diffDown;\n	\n	if(length(colCenter.rgb) < 0.01 && outline == 0. && abs(horizontalTotal) + abs(verticalTotal) > .0 && !u_bIgnoreBlack)\n		discard;\n	\n	vec2 normal = vec2(\n        horizontalTotal*.5+.5,\n        verticalTotal*.5+.5\n	);\n	\n    gl_FragColor = vec4(\n		normal.x,\n		normal.y,\n        length(colCenter.rgb),\n        alphaCenter\n    );\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough vertex shader\n//\nattribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)\n//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n#extension GL_OES_standard_derivatives : enable\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vTexel; \n\nuniform bool u_bIgnoreBlack;\n\nfloat get_alpha(vec4 col){\n	return float(col.a != 0.);//(u_bIgnoreBlack || length(col.rgb) > 0.01) && \n}\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	    // 1. Calculate how the texture coordinates change across screen X and Y\n    vec2 dTexX = vec2(dFdx(v_vTexcoord.x), dFdx(v_vTexcoord.y));\n    vec2 dTexY = vec2(dFdy(v_vTexcoord.x), dFdy(v_vTexcoord.y));\n\n    // 2. shd_pal_html_sprite

gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nattribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n//********SETUP*********\n//The following variables must be populated specific to your project.\n#define TexturePageSize 2048. \n//Your texture page size. Make sure it's actually exporting at the size you \n//set it if things seem funky. \n#define ColorCount 64. \n//How tall is your color palette? You could set this to the tallest one and\n//leave it, but if you are experiencing performances issues, there are some\n//ways to make this work a bit more efficiently.\n#define PixelSize 1./TexturePageSize\n#define PalHeight ColorCount * PixelSize\n#define Transparent vec4(.0,.0,.0,.0)\n#define Tolerance 0.004\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nuniform sampler2D u_palTexture;\nuniform vec4 u_Uvs;\nuniform float u_paletteId;\nuniform vec2 u_pixelSize;\n\nvec4 findAltColor(vec4 inCol, vec2 corner)\n{\n  if(inCol.a == 0.) return Transparent;\n  \n  float dist;\n  vec2 testPos;\n  vec4 leftCol;\n  for(float i=0.; i < PalHeight; i+=PixelSize)\n  {\n    testPos = vec2(corner.x,corner.y+i);\n    leftCol = texture2D( u_palTexture, testPos);\n    dist = distance(leftCol,inCol);\n    if(dist <= Tolerance)\n    {\n			testPos = vec2(corner.x + u_pixelSize.x * floor(u_paletteId + 1.0), corner.y + i);\n            return mix(texture2D(u_palTexture, vec2(testPos.x - u_pixelSize.x, testPos.y)), texture2D(u_palTexture, testPos), fract(u_paletteId));\n    }\n  }\n  return inCol;\n}\n\nvoid main()\n{\n  vec4 col = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n  DoAlphaTest( col );\n  col = findAltColor(col, u_Uvs.xy);\n  gl_FragColor = v_vColour * col;\n}\n\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nattribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n//********SETUP*********\n//The following variables must be populated specific to your project.\n#define TexturePageSize 2048. \n//Your texture page size. shd_pal_swapper
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n#define Transparent vec4(.0,.0,.0,.0)\n#define Tolerance 0.004 \n//If you feel like your colors should be matching but aren't, increase this number a bit.\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nuniform sampler2D u_palTexture;\nuniform vec4 u_Uvs;\nuniform float u_paletteId;\nuniform vec2 u_pixelSize;\n\nvec4 findAltColor(vec4 inCol, vec2 corner)\n{\n    if(inCol.a == 0.) return Transparent;\n    \n    float dist;\n    vec2 testPos;\n    vec4 leftCol;\n    for(float i = corner.y; i < u_Uvs.w; i+=u_pixelSize.y )\n    {\n			testPos = vec2(corner.x,i);\n      leftCol = texture2D( u_palTexture, testPos);\n        \n			dist = distance(leftCol,inCol);\n\n			if(dist < Tolerance)\n      {\n				testPos = vec2(corner.x + u_pixelSize.x * floor(u_paletteId + 1.0), i);\n        return mix(texture2D(u_palTexture, vec2(testPos.x - u_pixelSize.x, testPos.y)), texture2D(u_palTexture, testPos), fract(u_paletteId));\n      }\n    }\n    return inCol;\n}\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	vec4 col = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n	DoAlphaTest( col );\n	col = findAltColor(col, u_Uvs.xy);\n	gl_FragColor = v_vColour * col;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n#define Transparent vec4(.0,.0,.0,.0)\n#define Tolerance 0.004 \n//If you feel like your colors should be matching but aren't, increase this number a bit.\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nuniform sampler2D u_palTexture;\nuniform vec4 u_Uvs;\nuniform float u_paletteId;\nuniform vec2 u_pixelSize;\n\nvec4 findAltColor(vec4 inCol, vec2 corner)\n{\n    if(inCol.a == 0.) return Transparent;\n    \n    float dist;\n    vec2 testPos;\n    vec4 leftCol;\n    for(float i = corner.y; i < u_Uvs.w; i+=u_pixelSize.y )\n    {\n			testPos = vec2(corner.x,i);\n      leftCol = texture2D( u_palTexture, testPos);\n        \n			dist = distance(leftCol,inCol);\n\n			if(dist < Tolerance)\n      {\n				testPos = vec2(corner.x + u_pixelSize.x * floor(u_paletteId + 1.0), i);\n        return mix(texture2D(u_palTexture, vec2(testPos.x - u_pixelSize.x, testPos.y)), texture2D(u_palTexture, testPos), fract(u_paletteId));\n      }\n    }\n    return inCol;\n}\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	vec4 col = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n	DoAlphaTest( col );\n	col = findAltColor(col, u_Uvs.xy);\n	gl_FragColor = v_vColour * col;\n}\n
shd_perlin_noise_glsl_es
#define LOWPREC lowp\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\n#if __VERSION__ >= 130\n	#define attribute in\n	#define varying out\n#endif\n\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 tex_coord;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * vec4(in_Position.xyz, 1.0);\n    tex_coord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n// Reference - https://adrianb.io/2014/08/09/perlinnoise.html\n\nprecision highp float;\n\n#if __VERSION__ >= 130\n	out vec4 frag_color;\n	#define varying in\n	#define texture2D texture\n#else\n	#define frag_color gl_FragColor\n#endif\n\n#define M_PI   3.1415926535897932384626433832795\n\nfloat fade(float t)\n{\n    return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0);\n}\n\nfloat grad(int hash, float x, float y, float z)\n{\n    float h = mod(float(hash), 16.0);\n    float u = h < 8.0 ? x : y;\n\n    float v;\n\n    if(h < 4.0)\n        v = y;\n    else if(h == 12.0 || h == 14.0)\n        v = x;\n    else\n        v = z;\n\n    return (mod(h, 2.0) == 0.0 ? u : -u) + (mod(h, 3.0) < 2.0 ? v : -v);\n}\n\nfloat perlin(vec3 pos, int[256] table)\n{\n    int xi = int(mod(pos.x, 256.0));\n    int yi = int(mod(pos.y, 256.0));\n    int zi = int(mod(pos.z, 256.0));\n\n	int xj = int(mod(pos.x + 1.0, 256.0));\n	int yj = int(mod(pos.y + 1.0, 256.0));\n	int zj = int(mod(pos.z + 1.0, 256.0));\n\n	int aaa = table[table[table[xi] + yi] + zi];\n    int aba = table[table[table[xi] + yj] + zi];\n    int aab = table[table[table[xi] + yi] + zj];\n    int abb = table[table[table[xi] + yj] + zj];\n    int baa = table[table[table[xj] + yi] + zi];\n    int bba = table[table[table[xj] + yj] + zi];\n    int bab = table[table[table[xj] + yi] + zj];\n    int bbb = table[table[table[xj] + yj] + zj];\n\n    float xf = fract(pos.x);\n    float yf = fract(pos.y);\n    float zf = fract(pos.z);\n\n    float u = fade(xf);\n    float v = fade(yf);\n    float w = fade(zf);\n\n    float x1 = mix(grad(aaa, xf, yf,       zf), grad(baa, xf - 1.0, yf,       zf), u);\n    float y1 = mix(grad(aba, xf, yf - 1.0, zf), grad(bba, xf - 1.0, yf - 1.0, zf), u);\n    float z1 = mix(x1, y1, v);\n\n    float x2 = mix(grad(aab, xf, yf,       zf - 1.0), grad(bab, xf - 1.0, yf,       zf - 1.0), u);\n    float y2 = mix(grad(abb, xf, yf - 1.0, zf - 1.0), grad(bbb, xf - 1.0, yf - 1.0, zf - 1.0), u);\n    float z2 = mix (x2, y2, v);\n\n	return (mix(z1, z2, w) + 1.0) * 0.5;\n}\n\nvarying vec2 tex_coord;\n\nuniform float u_seed;\nuniform int u_table[256];\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	float value = perlin(vec3(sin(abs(tex_coord - 0.5) * M_PI * 0.5) * u_seed, fract(u_seed) * 1.387), u_table);\n    frag_color = vec4(vec3(value), 1.0);\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\n#if __VERSION__ >= 130\n	#define attribute in\n	#define varying out\n#endif\n\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 tex_coord;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * vec4(in_Position.xyz, 1.0);\n    tex_coord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n// Reference - https://adrianb.io/2014/08/09/perlinnoise.html\n\nprecision highp float;\n\n#if __VERSION__ >= 130\n	out vec4 frag_color;\n	#define varying in\n	#define texture2D texture\n#else\n	#define frag_color gl_FragColor\n#endif\n\n#define M_PI   3.1415926535897932384626433832795\n\nfloat fade(float t)\n{\n    return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0);\n}\n\nfloat grad(int hash, float x, float y, float z)\n{\n    float h = mod(float(hash), 16.0);\n    float u = h < 8.0 ? x : y;\n\n    float v;\n\n    if(h < 4.0)\n        v = y;\n    else if(h == 12.0 || h == 14.0)\n        v = x;\n    else\n        v = z;\n\n    return (mod(h, 2.0) == 0.0 ? u : -u) + (mod(h, 3.0) < 2.0 ? v : -v);\n}\n\nfloat perlin(vec3 pos, int[256] table)\n{\n    int xi = int(mod(pos.x, 256.0));\n    int yi = int(mod(pos.y, 256.0));\n    int zi = int(mod(pos.z, 256.0));\n\n	int xj = int(mod(pos.x + 1.0, 256.0));\n	int yj = int(mod(pos.y + 1.0, 256.0));\n	int zj = int(mod(pos.z + 1.0, 256.0));\n\n	int aaa = table[table[table[xi] + yi] + zi];\n    int aba = table[table[table[xi] + yj] + zi];\n    int aab = table[table[table[xi] + yi] + zj];\n    int abb = table[table[table[xi] + yj] + zj];\n    int baa = table[table[table[xj] + yi] + zi];\n    int bba = table[table[table[xj] + yj] + zi];\n    int bab = table[table[table[xj] + yi] + zj];\n    int bbb = table[table[table[xj] + yj] + zj];\n\n    float xf = fract(pos.x);\n    float yf = fract(pos.y);\n    float zf = fract(pos.z);\n\n    float u = fade(xf);\n    float v = fade(yf);\n    float w = fade(zf);\n\n    float x1 = mix(grad(aaa, xf, yf,       zf), grad(baa, xf - 1.0, yf,       zf), u);\n    float y1 = mix(grad(aba, xf, yf - 1.0, zf), grad(bba, xf - 1.0, yf - 1.0, zf), u);\n    float z1 = mix(x1, y1, v);\n\n    float x2 = mix(grad(aab, xf, yf,       zf - 1.0), grad(bab, xf - 1.0, yf,       zf - 1.0), u);\n    float y2 = mix(grad(abb, xf, yf - 1.0, zf - 1.0), grad(bbb, xf - 1.0, yf - 1.0, zf - 1.0), u);\n    float z2 = mix (x2, y2, v);\n\n	return (mix(z1, z2, w) + 1.0) * 0.5;\n}\n\nvarying vec2 tex_coord;\n\nuniform float u_seed;\nuniform int u_table[256];\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	float value = perlin(vec3(sin(abs(tex_coord - 0.5) * M_PI * 0.5) * u_seed, fract(u_seed) * 1.387), u_table);\n    frag_color = vec4(vec3(value), 1.0);\n}\n

gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough vertex shader\n//\nattribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)\n//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vPos;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n	v_vPos = in_Position.xy;\n	\n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough fragment shader\n//\n#define Tolerance 0.004 \n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vPos;\n\nuniform vec2 u_vScreenSize;\nuniform vec2 u_vTexel;\n\nuniform sampler2D u_sBloodTexture;\n\n\n\nuniform sampler2D u_palTexture;\nuniform vec4 u_Uvs;\nuniform vec2 u_paletteId;\nuniform vec2 u_pixelSize;\n\nvec4 findAltColor(vec3 inCol, vec2 corner, float pal_id)\n{\n    float dist;\n    vec2 testPos;\n    vec3 leftCol;\n    for(float i = corner.y; i < u_Uvs.w; i+=u_pixelSize.y )\n    {\n			testPos = vec2(corner.x,i);\n      leftCol = texture2D( u_palTexture, testPos).rgb;\n        \n			dist = distance(leftCol,inCol);\n\n			if(dist < Tolerance)\n      {\n				testPos = vec2(corner.x + u_pixelSize.x * floor(pal_id + 1.0), i);\n        return mix(texture2D(u_palTexture, vec2(testPos.x - u_pixelSize.x, testPos.y)), texture2D(u_palTexture, testPos), fract(pal_id));\n      }\n    }\n    return vec4(inCol,1.);\n}\n\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	vec4 wall_sample = v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );\n    vec4 blood_sample = texture2D( u_sBloodTexture, v_vTexcoord );\n	\n	if(blood_sample.a < .5 || wall_sample.a < .5)\n		discard;\n	\n	float right = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord + vec2(u_vTexel.x*2., 0.)).a;\n	float left = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord - vec2(u_vTexel.x*2., 0.)).a;\n	float down = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord + vec2(0.,u_vTexel.y*2.)).a;\n	float up = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord - vec2(0.,u_vTexel.y*2.)).a;\n	\n	float edge = 1. - min(min(right,left),min(down,up));\n	\n	gl_FragColor = findAltColor(wall_sample.rgb, u_Uvs.xy, mix(u_paletteId.x,u_paletteId.y,edge) );\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough vertex shader\n//\nattribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)\n//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vPos;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n    \n	v_vPos = in_Position.xy;\n	\n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
