pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough vertex shader\n//\nattribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)\n//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\nvarying vec3 v_vPosition;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    v_vPosition = in_Position;\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * vec4(v_vPosition.x, v_vPosition.y, v_vPosition.z, 1.0);\n    \n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\n//\n// Simple passthrough fragment shader\n//\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\nvarying vec3 v_vPosition;\n\nuniform vec4 u_ClipRect;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n	vec4 col = v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );\n	col.a *= float(v_vPosition.x >= u_ClipRect[0] && v_vPosition.y >= u_ClipRect[1] && v_vPosition.x < u_ClipRect[2] && v_vPosition.y < u_ClipRect[3]);\n    gl_FragColor = col;\n}\n
shFog
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nuniform sampler2D u_fogTex;\n\nuniform float u_time;\nuniform float u_radiusX;\nuniform float u_radiusY;\nuniform float u_distortAmp;\nuniform float u_fade;\nuniform float u_density;\n\nuniform vec2 u_center;\nuniform vec2 u_resolution;\nuniform vec2 u_camPos;\n\nfloat bayer4x4(vec2 pos) {\n    mat4 m = mat4(\n         0.0, 12.0,  3.0, 15.0,\n         8.0,  4.0, 11.0,  7.0,\n         2.0, 14.0,  1.0, 13.0,\n        10.0,  6.0,  9.0,  5.0\n    ) / 16.0;\n    int xi = int(mod(pos.x, 4.0));\n    int yi = int(mod(pos.y, 4.0));\n    return m[xi][yi];\n}\n\nvoid main() {\n    vec2 screenPos = v_vTexcoord * u_resolution;\n    vec2 worldPos  = screenPos + u_camPos;\n    vec2 local     = worldPos - u_center;\n\n    float warp =\n        sin(radians(local.x * 23.0) + radians(u_time)) +\n        sin(radians(local.y * 19.0) + radians(u_time * 1.4));\n    warp *= u_distortAmp;\n\n    float effRX = u_radiusX + warp;\n    float effRY = u_radiusY + warp;\n\n    if (effRX <= 0.0 || effRY <= 0.0) discard;\n\n    float dist2 =\n        (local.x * local.x) / (effRX * effRX) +\n        (local.y * local.y) / (effRY * effRY);\n\n    if (dist2 > 1.0) discard;\n\n    // world-anchored dither\n    float dither    = bayer4x4(worldPos);\n    float threshold = min(u_fade, clamp((1.0 - dist2) * u_density, 0.0, 1.0));\n    if (dither >= threshold) discard;\n\n    vec4 col = texture2D(u_fogTex, v_vTexcoord);\n    gl_FragColor = col * v_vColour;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nuniform sampler2D u_fogTex;\n\nuniform float u_time;\nuniform float u_radiusX;\nuniform float u_radiusY;\nuniform float u_distortAmp;\nuniform float u_fade;\nuniform float u_density;\n\nuniform vec2 u_center;\nuniform vec2 u_resolution;\nuniform vec2 u_camPos;\n\nfloat bayer4x4(vec2 pos) {\n    mat4 m = mat4(\n         0.0, 12.0,  3.0, 15.0,\n         8.0,  4.0, 11.0,  7.0,\n         2.0, 14.0,  1.0, 13.0,\n        10.0,  6.0,  9.0,  5.0\n    ) / 16.0;\n    int xi = int(mod(pos.x, 4.0));\n    int yi = int(mod(pos.y, 4.0));\n    return m[xi][yi];\n}\n\nvoid main() {\n    vec2 screenPos = v_vTexcoord * u_resolution;\n    vec2 worldPos  = screenPos + u_camPos;\n    vec2 local     = worldPos - u_center;\n\n    float warp =\n        sin(radians(local.x * 23.0) + radians(u_time)) +\n        sin(radians(local.y * 19.0) + radians(u_time * 1.4));\n    warp *= u_distortAmp;\n\n    float effRX = u_radiusX + warp;\n    float effRY = u_radiusY + warp;\n\n    if (effRX <= 0.0 || effRY <= 0.0) discard;\n\n    float dist2 =\n        (local.x * local.x) / (effRX * effRX) +\n        (local.y * local.y) / (effRY * effRY);\n\n    if (dist2 > 1.0) discard;\n\n    // world-anchored dither\n    float dither    = bayer4x4(worldPos);\n    float threshold = min(u_fade, clamp((1.0 - dist2) * u_density, 0.0, 1.0));\n    if (dither >= threshold) discard;\n\n    vec4 col = texture2D(u_fogTex, v_vTexcoord);\n    gl_FragColor = col * v_vColour;\n}\n
shOcean
#define LOWPREC lowp\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\n\nvoid main() {\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * vec4(in_Position.xyz, 1.0);\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\n#if __VERSION__ >= 130\n    out vec4 frag_color;\n    #define varying in\n    #define texture2D texture\n#else\n    #define frag_color gl_FragColor\n#endif\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\n\nuniform float u_time;\nuniform float u_speed;\nuniform float u_spread;\nuniform float u_depth_scale;\nuniform float u_ground_uv;\nuniform float u_falloff;\nuniform float u_light_spread;\nuniform float u_is_under; // 1.0 = under draw, 0.0 = upper draw\nuniform float u_thresh0;\nuniform float u_thresh1;\nuniform float u_thresh2;\nuniform float u_thresh3;\nuniform vec3  u_color0;\nuniform vec3  u_color1;\nuniform vec3  u_color2;\nuniform vec2  u_player_uv;\nuniform float u_shadow_radius;\nuniform vec2  u_vendor_uv;\nuniform float u_vendor_radius;\nuniform float u_aspect;\nuniform vec2  u_offering0_uv;\nuniform float u_offering0_radius;\nuniform vec2  u_offering1_uv;\nuniform float u_offering1_radius;\nuniform vec2  u_offering2_uv;\nuniform float u_offering2_radius;\n\nfloat hash(vec2 p) {\n    return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);\n}\n\nfloat vnoise(vec2 p) {\n    vec2 i = floor(p);\n    vec2 f = fract(p);\n    vec2 u = f * f * f * (f * (f * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0);\n    return mix(mix(hash(i + vec2(0,0)), hash(i + vec2(1,0)), u.x),\n               mix(hash(i + vec2(0,1)), hash(i + vec2(1,1)), u.x), u.y);\n}\n\nfloat fbm(vec2 p) {\n    vec2 warp = vec2(vnoise(p * 0.7 + vec2(1.7, 9.2)),\n                     vnoise(p * 0.7 + vec2(8.3, 2.8)));\n    p += warp * 0.8;\n    float v = 0.0, a = 0.5;\n    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n        v += a * (vnoise(p) * 2.0 - 1.0);\n        p = p * 2.0 + vec2(5.3, 1.7);\n        a *= 0.42;\n    }\n    return clamp(v * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0);\n}\n\nvoid main() {\n    float sy = v_vTexcoord.y;\n    float g  = u_ground_uv;\n\n    // hard clip each call to its own side to prevent seam bleed\n    if (u_is_under > 0.5 && sy > g) { frag_color = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0); return; }\n    if (u_is_under < 0.5 && sy < g) { frag_color = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0); return; }\n\n    float dist;\n    float scale;\n\n    if (sy <= g) {\n        dist    = (g - sy) / g;\n        float t = 1.0 - dist;\n        scale   = min(1.0 / (t * t + 0.02), 80.0);\n    } else {\n        dist    = (sy - g) / (1.0 - g);\n        scale   = min(1.0 / (1.0 - dist + 0.03), 5.0);\n    }\n\n    float wx = (v_vTexcoord.x - 0.5) * scale * u_spread;\n    float wz = scale * u_depth_scale - u_time * u_speed;\n\n    float ripple = sin(wz * 1.1 - u_time * u_speed * 4.0) * 0.45 * (1.0 - dist);\n    wx += ripple;\n\n    float n = fbm(vec2(wx, wz));\n\n    float dx   = abs(v_vTexcoord.x - 0.5);\n    float bias = dist * u_falloff + dx * u_light_spread;\n    float val  = clamp(n - bias, 0.0, 1.0);\n\n    float sh = 1.0;\n    if (u_shadow_radius > 0.0) {\n        float cy   = u_player_uv.y + (u_ground_uv - u_player_uv.y) * 0.12;\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_player_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  =  v_vTexcoord.y - cy;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_shadow_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_shadow_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (u_vendor_radius > 0.0) {\n        float cy   = u_vendor_uv.y + (u_ground_uv - u_vendor_uv.y) * 0.12;\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_vendor_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  =  v_vTexcoord.y - cy;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_vendor_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_vendor_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (u_offering0_radius > 0.0) {\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_offering0_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  = (v_vTexcoord.y - u_offering0_uv.y) * 7.0;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_offering0_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_offering0_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (u_offering1_radius > 0.0) {\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_offering1_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  = (v_vTexcoord.y - u_offering1_uv.y) * 7.0;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_offering1_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_offering1_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (u_offering2_radius > 0.0) {\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_offering2_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  = (v_vTexcoord.y - u_offering2_uv.y) * 7.0;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_offering2_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_offering2_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (sh < 1.0) {\n        float dark = u_is_under < 0.5 ? 0.25 : 0.65;\n        val = val * mix(dark, 1.0, sh);\n    }\n\n    float graw       = fbm(vec2(wx * 1.5, wz * 4.0));\n    bool  glint_on   = graw > u_thresh2 && dist < 0.5 && val > u_thresh1;\n    bool  thresh_on  = val >= u_thresh2 && val < u_thresh3;\n\n    vec3 col;\n    if      (val < u_thresh0)           col = vec3(0.0);\n    else if (glint_on || thresh_on)     col = u_color2;\n    else if (val < u_thresh1)           col = u_color0;\n    else                                col = u_color1;\n\n    frag_color = vec4(col, 1.0);\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\n\nvoid main() {\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * vec4(in_Position.xyz, 1.0);\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\n#if __VERSION__ >= 130\n    out vec4 frag_color;\n    #define varying in\n    #define texture2D texture\n#else\n    #define frag_color gl_FragColor\n#endif\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\n\nuniform float u_time;\nuniform float u_speed;\nuniform float u_spread;\nuniform float u_depth_scale;\nuniform float u_ground_uv;\nuniform float u_falloff;\nuniform float u_light_spread;\nuniform float u_is_under; // 1.0 = under draw, 0.0 = upper draw\nuniform float u_thresh0;\nuniform float u_thresh1;\nuniform float u_thresh2;\nuniform float u_thresh3;\nuniform vec3  u_color0;\nuniform vec3  u_color1;\nuniform vec3  u_color2;\nuniform vec2  u_player_uv;\nuniform float u_shadow_radius;\nuniform vec2  u_vendor_uv;\nuniform float u_vendor_radius;\nuniform float u_aspect;\nuniform vec2  u_offering0_uv;\nuniform float u_offering0_radius;\nuniform vec2  u_offering1_uv;\nuniform float u_offering1_radius;\nuniform vec2  u_offering2_uv;\nuniform float u_offering2_radius;\n\nfloat hash(vec2 p) {\n    return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);\n}\n\nfloat vnoise(vec2 p) {\n    vec2 i = floor(p);\n    vec2 f = fract(p);\n    vec2 u = f * f * f * (f * (f * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0);\n    return mix(mix(hash(i + vec2(0,0)), hash(i + vec2(1,0)), u.x),\n               mix(hash(i + vec2(0,1)), hash(i + vec2(1,1)), u.x), u.y);\n}\n\nfloat fbm(vec2 p) {\n    vec2 warp = vec2(vnoise(p * 0.7 + vec2(1.7, 9.2)),\n                     vnoise(p * 0.7 + vec2(8.3, 2.8)));\n    p += warp * 0.8;\n    float v = 0.0, a = 0.5;\n    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n        v += a * (vnoise(p) * 2.0 - 1.0);\n        p = p * 2.0 + vec2(5.3, 1.7);\n        a *= 0.42;\n    }\n    return clamp(v * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0);\n}\n\nvoid main() {\n    float sy = v_vTexcoord.y;\n    float g  = u_ground_uv;\n\n    // hard clip each call to its own side to prevent seam bleed\n    if (u_is_under > 0.5 && sy > g) { frag_color = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0); return; }\n    if (u_is_under < 0.5 && sy < g) { frag_color = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0); return; }\n\n    float dist;\n    float scale;\n\n    if (sy <= g) {\n        dist    = (g - sy) / g;\n        float t = 1.0 - dist;\n        scale   = min(1.0 / (t * t + 0.02), 80.0);\n    } else {\n        dist    = (sy - g) / (1.0 - g);\n        scale   = min(1.0 / (1.0 - dist + 0.03), 5.0);\n    }\n\n    float wx = (v_vTexcoord.x - 0.5) * scale * u_spread;\n    float wz = scale * u_depth_scale - u_time * u_speed;\n\n    float ripple = sin(wz * 1.1 - u_time * u_speed * 4.0) * 0.45 * (1.0 - dist);\n    wx += ripple;\n\n    float n = fbm(vec2(wx, wz));\n\n    float dx   = abs(v_vTexcoord.x - 0.5);\n    float bias = dist * u_falloff + dx * u_light_spread;\n    float val  = clamp(n - bias, 0.0, 1.0);\n\n    float sh = 1.0;\n    if (u_shadow_radius > 0.0) {\n        float cy   = u_player_uv.y + (u_ground_uv - u_player_uv.y) * 0.12;\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_player_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  =  v_vTexcoord.y - cy;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_shadow_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_shadow_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (u_vendor_radius > 0.0) {\n        float cy   = u_vendor_uv.y + (u_ground_uv - u_vendor_uv.y) * 0.12;\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_vendor_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  =  v_vTexcoord.y - cy;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_vendor_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_vendor_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (u_offering0_radius > 0.0) {\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_offering0_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  = (v_vTexcoord.y - u_offering0_uv.y) * 7.0;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_offering0_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_offering0_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (u_offering1_radius > 0.0) {\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_offering1_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  = (v_vTexcoord.y - u_offering1_uv.y) * 7.0;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_offering1_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_offering1_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (u_offering2_radius > 0.0) {\n        float pdx  = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_offering2_uv.x) * u_aspect;\n        float pdy  = (v_vTexcoord.y - u_offering2_uv.y) * 7.0;\n        float pd   = length(vec2(pdx, pdy));\n        float wave = sin(atan(pdy, pdx) * 5.0 + u_time * 3.0) * u_offering2_radius * 0.15;\n        sh = min(sh, smoothstep(0.0, u_offering2_radius + wave, pd));\n    }\n    if (sh < 1.0) {\n        float dark = u_is_under < 0.5 ? 0.25 : 0.65;\n        val = val * mix(dark, 1.0, sh);\n    }\n\n    float graw       = fbm(vec2(wx * 1.5, wz * 4.0));\n    bool  glint_on   = graw > u_thresh2 && dist < 0.5 && val > u_thresh1;\n    bool  thresh_on  = val >= u_thresh2 && val < u_thresh3;\n\n    vec3 col;\n    if      (val < u_thresh0)           col = vec3(0.0);\n    else if (glint_on || thresh_on)     col = u_color2;\n    else if (val < u_thresh1)           col = u_color0;\n    else                                col = u_color1;\n\n    frag_color = vec4(col, 1.0);\n}\n
shPerlinBloodBG
#define LOWPREC lowp\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n\n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\n\nuniform float u_time;\nuniform vec2 u_resolution;\nuniform float u_scrollY;\nuniform float u_noiseCellSize;\nuniform float u_noiseSpeed;\nuniform float u_noiseAmount;\nuniform float u_threshold1;\nuniform float u_threshold2;\nuniform float u_marginPx;\nuniform vec3 u_colorNear;\nuniform vec3 u_colorMid;\nuniform vec3 u_colorFar;\n\nvec4 mod289(vec4 x) { return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0; }\nvec4 permute(vec4 x) { return mod289(((x * 34.0) + 1.0) * x); }\nvec4 taylorInvSqrt(vec4 r) { return 1.79284291400159 - 0.85373472095314 * r; }\nvec2 fade(vec2 t) { return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0); }\n\nfloat cnoise(vec2 P)\n{\n    vec4 Pi = floor(P.xyxy) + vec4(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0);\n    vec4 Pf = fract(P.xyxy) - vec4(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0);\n    Pi = mod289(Pi);\n    vec4 ix = Pi.xzxz;\n    vec4 iy = Pi.yyww;\n    vec4 fx = Pf.xzxz;\n    vec4 fy = Pf.yyww;\n    vec4 i = permute(permute(ix) + iy);\n    vec4 gx = 2.0 * fract(i * 0.0243902439) - 1.0;\n    vec4 gy = abs(gx) - 0.5;\n    vec4 tx = floor(gx + 0.5);\n    gx = gx - tx;\n    vec2 g00 = vec2(gx.x, gy.x);\n    vec2 g10 = vec2(gx.y, gy.y);\n    vec2 g01 = vec2(gx.z, gy.z);\n    vec2 g11 = vec2(gx.w, gy.w);\n    vec4 norm = taylorInvSqrt(vec4(dot(g00, g00), dot(g01, g01), dot(g10, g10), dot(g11, g11)));\n    g00 *= norm.x;\n    g01 *= norm.y;\n    g10 *= norm.z;\n    g11 *= norm.w;\n    float n00 = dot(g00, vec2(fx.x, fy.x));\n    float n10 = dot(g10, vec2(fx.y, fy.y));\n    float n01 = dot(g01, vec2(fx.z, fy.z));\n    float n11 = dot(g11, vec2(fx.w, fy.w));\n    vec2 fade_xy = fade(Pf.xy);\n    vec2 n_x = mix(vec2(n00, n01), vec2(n10, n11), fade_xy.x);\n    float n_xy = mix(n_x.x, n_x.y, fade_xy.y);\n    return 2.3 * n_xy;\n}\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec2 uv = v_vTexcoord;\n    float halfWidth = max(u_resolution.x * 0.5 - u_marginPx, 1.0);\n    float dist = abs(uv.x * u_resolution.x - u_resolution.x * 0.5) / halfWidth;\n\n    vec2 pixelPos = vec2(uv.x * u_resolution.x, uv.y * u_resolution.y + u_scrollY);\n    vec2 noiseUV = pixelPos / u_noiseCellSize;\n\n    float n1 = cnoise(noiseUV + vec2(0.0, u_time * u_noiseSpeed));\n    float n2 = cnoise(noiseUV * 2.3 - vec2(u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 0.6, 0.0));\n    float n3 = cnoise(noiseUV * 5.0 + vec2(u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 1.3, u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 0.4));\n    float n4 = cnoise(noiseUV * 11.0 - vec2(u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 0.8, u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 0.5));\n    float noise = n1 * 0.5 + n2 * 0.25 + n3 * 0.15 + n4 * 0.1;\n\n    float edge = dist + noise * u_noiseAmount;\n\n    vec3 col = u_colorNear;\n    if (edge >= u_threshold1) col = u_colorMid;\n    if (edge >= u_threshold2) col = u_colorFar;\n\n    gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;\n\n    v_vColour = in_Colour;\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\n\nuniform float u_time;\nuniform vec2 u_resolution;\nuniform float u_scrollY;\nuniform float u_noiseCellSize;\nuniform float u_noiseSpeed;\nuniform float u_noiseAmount;\nuniform float u_threshold1;\nuniform float u_threshold2;\nuniform float u_marginPx;\nuniform vec3 u_colorNear;\nuniform vec3 u_colorMid;\nuniform vec3 u_colorFar;\n\nvec4 mod289(vec4 x) { return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0; }\nvec4 permute(vec4 x) { return mod289(((x * 34.0) + 1.0) * x); }\nvec4 taylorInvSqrt(vec4 r) { return 1.79284291400159 - 0.85373472095314 * r; }\nvec2 fade(vec2 t) { return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0); }\n\nfloat cnoise(vec2 P)\n{\n    vec4 Pi = floor(P.xyxy) + vec4(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0);\n    vec4 Pf = fract(P.xyxy) - vec4(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0);\n    Pi = mod289(Pi);\n    vec4 ix = Pi.xzxz;\n    vec4 iy = Pi.yyww;\n    vec4 fx = Pf.xzxz;\n    vec4 fy = Pf.yyww;\n    vec4 i = permute(permute(ix) + iy);\n    vec4 gx = 2.0 * fract(i * 0.0243902439) - 1.0;\n    vec4 gy = abs(gx) - 0.5;\n    vec4 tx = floor(gx + 0.5);\n    gx = gx - tx;\n    vec2 g00 = vec2(gx.x, gy.x);\n    vec2 g10 = vec2(gx.y, gy.y);\n    vec2 g01 = vec2(gx.z, gy.z);\n    vec2 g11 = vec2(gx.w, gy.w);\n    vec4 norm = taylorInvSqrt(vec4(dot(g00, g00), dot(g01, g01), dot(g10, g10), dot(g11, g11)));\n    g00 *= norm.x;\n    g01 *= norm.y;\n    g10 *= norm.z;\n    g11 *= norm.w;\n    float n00 = dot(g00, vec2(fx.x, fy.x));\n    float n10 = dot(g10, vec2(fx.y, fy.y));\n    float n01 = dot(g01, vec2(fx.z, fy.z));\n    float n11 = dot(g11, vec2(fx.w, fy.w));\n    vec2 fade_xy = fade(Pf.xy);\n    vec2 n_x = mix(vec2(n00, n01), vec2(n10, n11), fade_xy.x);\n    float n_xy = mix(n_x.x, n_x.y, fade_xy.y);\n    return 2.3 * n_xy;\n}\n\nvoid main()\n{\n    vec2 uv = v_vTexcoord;\n    float halfWidth = max(u_resolution.x * 0.5 - u_marginPx, 1.0);\n    float dist = abs(uv.x * u_resolution.x - u_resolution.x * 0.5) / halfWidth;\n\n    vec2 pixelPos = vec2(uv.x * u_resolution.x, uv.y * u_resolution.y + u_scrollY);\n    vec2 noiseUV = pixelPos / u_noiseCellSize;\n\n    float n1 = cnoise(noiseUV + vec2(0.0, u_time * u_noiseSpeed));\n    float n2 = cnoise(noiseUV * 2.3 - vec2(u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 0.6, 0.0));\n    float n3 = cnoise(noiseUV * 5.0 + vec2(u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 1.3, u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 0.4));\n    float n4 = cnoise(noiseUV * 11.0 - vec2(u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 0.8, u_time * u_noiseSpeed * 0.5));\n    float noise = n1 * 0.5 + n2 * 0.25 + n3 * 0.15 + n4 * 0.1;\n\n    float edge = dist + noise * u_noiseAmount;\n\n    vec3 col = u_colorNear;\n    if (edge >= u_threshold1) col = u_colorMid;\n    if (edge >= u_threshold2) col = u_colorFar;\n\n    gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);\n}\n
shSplash
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\n#if __VERSION__ >= 130\n    out vec4 frag_color;\n    #define varying in\n    #define texture2D texture\n#else\n    #define frag_color gl_FragColor\n#endif\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\n\nuniform float u_time;\nuniform vec4  u_uv_bounds;     // atlas (u0, v0, u1, v1) of sprite content\nuniform float u_waterline_v;   // atlas V of water line\nuniform float u_splash_height; // atlas V height of splash band\nuniform vec3  u_color;\n\nvoid main() {\n    vec4 spr = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n    if (spr.a < 0.5) { frag_color = vec4(0.0); return; }\n\n    float vy  = v_vTexcoord.y;\n    float top = u_waterline_v - u_splash_height;\n\n    if (vy < top || vy > u_waterline_v) { frag_color = vec4(0.0); return; }\n\n    // Normalize X across sprite content width (handles flipped sprites fine)\n    float nx = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_uv_bounds.x) / (u_uv_bounds.z - u_uv_bounds.x);\n\n    // t: 0 at top of splash band, 1 at water line\n    float t = (vy - top) / u_splash_height;\n\n    float wave = sin(nx * 30.0 + u_time * 4.0) * 0.18\n               + sin(nx * 13.0 - u_time * 2.5) * 0.09;\n\n    float foam = smoothstep(0.45 + wave, 0.72 + wave, t);\n\n    frag_color = vec4(u_color, foam * spr.a);\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nprecision highp float;\n\n#if __VERSION__ >= 130\n    out vec4 frag_color;\n    #define varying in\n    #define texture2D texture\n#else\n    #define frag_color gl_FragColor\n#endif\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\n\nuniform float u_time;\nuniform vec4  u_uv_bounds;     // atlas (u0, v0, u1, v1) of sprite content\nuniform float u_waterline_v;   // atlas V of water line\nuniform float u_splash_height; // atlas V height of splash band\nuniform vec3  u_color;\n\nvoid main() {\n    vec4 spr = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n    if (spr.a < 0.5) { frag_color = vec4(0.0); return; }\n\n    float vy  = v_vTexcoord.y;\n    float top = u_waterline_v - u_splash_height;\n\n    if (vy < top || vy > u_waterline_v) { frag_color = vec4(0.0); return; }\n\n    // Normalize X across sprite content width (handles flipped sprites fine)\n    float nx = (v_vTexcoord.x - u_uv_bounds.x) / (u_uv_bounds.z - u_uv_bounds.x);\n\n    // t: 0 at top of splash band, 1 at water line\n    float t = (vy - top) / u_splash_height;\n\n    float wave = sin(nx * 30.0 + u_time * 4.0) * 0.18\n               + sin(nx * 13.0 - u_time * 2.5) * 0.09;\n\n    float foam = smoothstep(0.45 + wave, 0.72 + wave, t);\n\n    frag_color = vec4(u_color, foam * spr.a);\n}\n
shWallCrumble
#define LOWPREC lowp\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main() {\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n    v_vColour   = in_Colour;\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * vec4(in_Position.xyz, 1.0);\n}\n
precision mediump float;\n#define LOWPREC lowp\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main() {\n    vec4 col = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord) * v_vColour;\n    if (col.a < 0.01) discard;\n    gl_FragColor = col;\n}\n
#version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0\n#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3\n#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4\n#define	MATRICES_MAX					5\n\nuniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; \n\nuniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;\nuniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;\nuniform float gm_FogStart;\nuniform float gm_RcpFogRange;\n\n#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8\n#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n\n\n//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8\nuniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range\nuniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1\n\nfloat CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)\n{\n	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;\n		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);\n		return fogfactor;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return 0.0;\n	}\n}\n\nvec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);\n	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)\n{\n	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;\n	float veclen = length(diffvec);\n	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise\n	float atten;\n	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs\n	{\n		atten = 0.0;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION\n	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:\n	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))\n	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)\n	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)\n	\n		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);\n		if (veclen > posrange.w)\n		{\n			atten = 0.0;\n		}	\n#else\n		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp\n#endif\n	}\n	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);\n	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);\n\n	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;\n}\n\nvec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)\n{\n	if (gm_LightingEnabled)\n	{\n		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space\n		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space\n		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);\n		vec3 ws_normal;\n		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;\n		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);\n\n		vec3 ws_pos;\n		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;\n\n		// Accumulate lighting from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;\n		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);\n		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;\n		return accumcol;\n	}\n	else\n	{\n		return vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main() {\n    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;\n    v_vColour   = in_Colour;\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * vec4(in_Position.xyz, 1.0);\n}\n

from different light types\n		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)\n		{\n			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);\n		}\n\n		accumcol *= vertexcolour;\n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1\nattribute vec3 in_Position;\nattribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;\nattribute vec4 in_Colour;\n\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nuniform float u_amplitude; // world-pixel displacement\nuniform float u_scale;     // spatial period in world pixels\nuniform vec2 u_camOffset;  // camera position to convert surface coords to world space\n\nvoid main() {\n    vec2 p = (in_Position.xy + u_camOffset) / u_scale;\n\n    float ox = sin(p.x + sin(p.y * 0.71)) * sin(p.y * 0.53 + p.x * 0.31);\n    float oy = cos(p.y + cos(p.x * 0.79)) * cos(p.x * 0.47 + p.y * 0.38);\n\n    vec2 warped = p + vec2(ox, oy) * 0.28;\n    float ox2   = sin(warped.x * 1.7) * 0.45;\n    float oy2   = cos(warped.y * 1.6) * 1.7) * 0.45;\n    float oy2   = cos(warped.y * 1.6) * 0.45;\n\n    vec2 disp = vec2(ox + ox2, oy + oy2) / 1.45 * u_amplitude;\n\n    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * #version 120\n#define LOWPREC \n#define lowp\n#define mediump\n#define highp\n#define precision\n// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes\n\nuniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;\n\nuniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;\nuniform vec4 gm_FogColour;\nuniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;\nuniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;\n\nvoid DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)\n{\n	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)\n	{\n		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)\n		{\n			discard;\n		}\n	}\n}\n\nvoid DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)\n{\n	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)\n	{\n		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); \n	}\n}\n\n#define _YY_GLSL_ 1\nvarying vec2 v_vTexcoord;\nvarying vec4 v_vColour;\n\nvoid main() {\n    gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);\n}\n
